# Pirate Island Asset Pack - 756 Generation Prompts

Source: `asset_ref_list_notion.csv`

Structure: 63 assets x 4 image variations x 3 modeling views = 756 prompts.

Global style rules for every prompt:

- Low-poly stylized pirate island Unity Asset Store pack.
- Simple colors, flat shaded surfaces, clean geometry.
- Realistic proportions and production-friendly scale.
- No additional decorative details beyond the asset function.
- Neutral background, centered single object, no text, no watermark.
- Use orthographic views: front, side, top.

Recommended generation workflow: generate each variation as a small set, compare silhouettes, then keep the best image gallery URL in Notion.
